  Klara在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个男婴，取名Alois Bauer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Anton把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Klara躺在床上出了一身虚汗。

  Klara难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Helene Bauer；Klara一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Anton把钟表送进当铺。Klara落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

  _effects: health -0.12 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

  _effects: cash 6 (失业救济的号码牌); condition; flag +unemployment_benefit; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “six kronen a month is granted without a written reason for d”_

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; condition; reputation 0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1941.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Klara的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Klara看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003341_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Franz Berg的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1941.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-ambulance` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Klara被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission); health 0.04999999999999999 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1941.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Franz Berg说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Klara站在原地看着Franz Berg拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1941.11** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  楼梯间的灯泡忽明忽暗，Klara让Helene Berg 2记下今晚进出院子的人。名单留在桌上没动，像一件没人认领的活。快到午夜，Helene Berg 2只说了一句，明早再核一遍。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1942.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Franz Berg没有答应，只说现在不合适。Klara把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005077_

  Klara和Emil Weiss在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Anton没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005369_

  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005369_

- **1942.01** `r14-hist.hiding-room::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hid-in-the-attic` tags: sheltered_person, rescue, hiding, repression, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Josef Weiss把Mitzi Stein带上阁楼，因为Mitzi Stein是犹太人，证件已经被注销；床垫铺在烟囱后面，白天不能咳嗽，夜里才能下楼用盆。Klara每天多买一份面包，把配给证上的名字改成“表亲”，Emil Weiss问起时说是乡下来养病的。第一周最难，楼梯一响，Mitzi Stein就把手按在墙上，等脚步过去。
  _effects: cash -10 (阁楼上的第三张床); food; flag +sheltered_a_jew; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

  Klara顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (走了很远的碎石路，脚底发疼); mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

  两个便衣跟着门房上楼，说要查一份“反动传单”。Klara把柜底的纸塞进Mitzi Stein送来的面粉袋，又抓了一把面盖住。Emil站在门口拖时间，说家里只有老人和孩子；便衣翻到米缸时，Klara的膝盖一直在抖。

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

  _effects: cash -18 (一麻袋钞票买一块面包); food; condition; flag +spent_savings_before_prices_rose_again; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; knowledge “prices are reposted twice a day”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Klara刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Emil Weiss一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Emil Weiss的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1944.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Josef Weiss和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  Klara只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

  Klara看Emil Weiss盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Emil Weiss愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Klara门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Klara打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。

  _effects: cash -4 (medicine); condition_

  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1948.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Josef在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.10** `vienna-historical-social-movement.relief-network/open-the-books-again` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara把账本摊在面粉袋上，一栏一栏念，念到某笔去向不明时屋里静了。老人把手套戴上又摘下，Helene Kral 2拿回自己那份登记，说先按墙上的数领。雪还在下，门一直开着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; reputation 0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.invite-to-theatre/two-gallery-tickets` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  在柜台前把话问出口，对方愣了一下，说星期天下午可以。两张楼座票一共两克朗二十赫勒，约在剧院侧门前见。对方把那叠乐谱往旁边推了推，说散场后可以沿环城道走一段。
  _effects: cash -2.2 (两张星期天轻歌剧楼座票); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

- **1949.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.repeat-at-bakery` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara在面包店排队时把听来的话又讲了一遍，添了“夜里常有男人上楼”一句。到傍晚，整栋楼都在说二楼女房客的事，连看门人都在门口站着看。二楼的门一直关着，Klara回家时才觉得那句话说得太响。
  _effects: reputation -0.06000000000000005_

- **1949.07** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1949.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1949.10** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Klara端着一只旧锅去敲Hermann Stein的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Hermann Stein让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Klara连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002154:child; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1949.11** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-church-steps` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Klara请同住的Maria Berg跑一趟，把Friedrich Berg 2约到教堂前的台阶上。石台阶还带着晨露，两人各占一级，把原信从头念完；念到“工钱”那处，Friedrich Berg 2说那是家里人的意思，不是自己的话。Klara把信纸折回原样，至少这一回没有隔着别人传话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002614:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146_

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.sickness-insurance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/employer-refused-stamp` tags: health, insurance, work, stamps, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara把保险册交给工头，让他盖厂里的章。工头说厂里已经统一办过，不用个人再办，把册子推回来。Klara后来才知道，厂里只给正式工办，临时工的名字没在名单上。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +insurance_stamp_refused; knowledge “the factory only enrols permanent workers in the sickness fu”_

- **1950.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara上楼敲了Rosa Moser 2的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Rosa Moser 2把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Klara能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1951.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Klara把报纸推到一边，和Anton Lechner聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Klara起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1951.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara请楼下的Elisabeth Huber白天来照看Josef Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Elisabeth Huber上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Klara下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Josef Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱)_

- **1951.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  楼道里正晾着几家洗过的衣服，Klara和Ferdinand Moser 2站在水滴下面说话，声音压得低。Ferdinand Moser 2说这栋楼里的住户不是头一回被这样要钱，先凑个口信比一个人硬顶强。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1951.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Klara下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1951.12** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.kahlenberg-sunday/kahlenberg-rain` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  走到半山就落雨，三人挤进酒农的棚子，把篮子里的面包和盐分完，吃食折合一克朗。Franz Huber 2把外套顶在头上先跑，Gustav Moser与Klara留在最后收篮子；雨点打在葡萄叶上，Klara的鞋袜湿到傍晚，脚趾发麻，谁也没急着走。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

- **1952.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/give-camp-bed-and-board` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara让Hermann Stein睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Klara把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002154:child; flag +kin_staying_over_

- **1952.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/leave-them-alone` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍完就找了个由头先走，说家里炉子还烧着，账只结了自己的那一杯。晚上回到楼里，缝纫女工在楼梯上碰见，只说那人挺有意思，没再多讲。老友第二天在印厂什么也没提，这事就搁在那里。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (自己那一杯咖啡); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002614:child; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003215:child; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.04** `kernel-civic.exile-departure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/civic-exile-refused` tags: politics, exile, migration, repression, station, vienna, urban, postwar
  她在护照窗口排到下午，Otto Stein把材料推回来，说缺少一份“无政治记录”的证明。她问去哪里开，Otto Stein说“去问你的警察局”；她走出市政厅时，天已经黑了，行李还在车站寄存处。她把行李票翻过来，背面记着朋友家的地址。
  _effects: flag +exit_visa_refused; cash -2 (排队时交的照片费和寄存费); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000823:child; knowledge “离境护照需要无政治记录证明，证明要去警察局开”_

- **1952.07** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Josef Winter在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Mitzi没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06000000000000005; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:008240; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1952.07** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara第一次真正坐在一张有权力的桌子后面。那一刻比想象中安静，也比想象中更让人舍不得起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008240_

  Klara和Anton Bauer在第一个酒棚就坐下了，要了一壶新酒，Katharina Bauer摸出一副磨损的纸牌。三人打到下午，太阳偏西也没再往上爬；Klara输了几个铜板，连酒带牌局一共一克朗五十赫勒，倒赢回一肚子笑话。回程的电车上，Klara靠着窗，酒意上来，差点坐过站。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (酒棚的新酒和牌局输的铜板); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002721:child; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1953.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.turned-back` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个)_

- **1953.06** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  有人喊罢工，有人喊谈判，Klara站到前排说先动才有得谈。保守派转身去拿大衣，Katharina Moser 2追出去又回来，说外头雪深，别把人推到对面去。会议没散，火气没降。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

  Mitzi在禁令上签了字，法警把纸贴在厂门上，说即日起不许聚众阻拦出入。Luise Kral把纠察队撤到街角，权力俱乐部的货车一辆辆开进厂区。Klara站在街角，看着货车卷起灰；Luise Kral说先守法，再谈别的，可谁都知道停工一天就少一天工钱。
  _effects: cash -6 (禁令贴在厂门上); condition; flag +union_obeyed_strike_injunction; relation colleague with person-luise-kral; knowledge “the injunction bans pickets within one block of the factory ”_

  _effects: organization_found 市民礼仪处 (state); position 公共礼仪官 @org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:008578; reputation 0.07999999999999996; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: flag +petition_signatures_lost; reputation -0.010000000000000009; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸和电车票); knowledge “签名纸要夹在硬纸板里，电车挤掉的一角找不回来”_

  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1957.06** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death

  _effects: death (肺炎)_

- **1934.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-flour-powder` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  药房的后门开着，一个穿军大衣的人按包卖药：退烧粉、止血粉、止痛粉。Mitzi买了三包，回家发现其中一包是面粉；再去找人，后门已经锁了。他把那包面粉倒进碗里，又倒回纸袋，没舍得扔。

  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

  _effects: mood -0.04_

  她本来已经走开了两步，又回头还了个价。摊主说这杯子是从一位老小姐的遗产里收来的，光收旧货的车钱就不止这些。Mitzi把三十赫勒排在摊布上，老头盯着看了一会儿，把杯子推过来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (还价后买下旧杯子); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1944.12** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Friedrich Moser把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Mitzi把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Mitzi在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1945.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  天色还灰着，Mitzi手里攥着几张折皱的证明。Ferdinand Huber让他念车皮上的号码，听见念对了，就朝装卸班一挥手，说先按日结钱，一天两克朗四十分。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org-wien-magistrat; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/posters-pasted` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的办事员把一叠防疫告示交给Mitzi，说浆糊要刷匀，别让风揭了角。Mitzi沿着市场街贴到天黑，手指被纸边割出细口。第二天，告示下面多了一行铅笔字：“水要煮开”。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

- **1946.08** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Gustav Kral抱着孩子连声道谢时，Mitzi已经弯着腰在咳。药房玻璃瓶上贴着褪色标签，喝下去满嘴甘草味；后半夜Mitzi听见自己牙齿打颤，炉子早灭了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002698:child_

- **1946.09** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-credit-full` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居帮你把半筐煤抬上楼，楼梯窄，煤块滚了两级。你蹲下去捡，指甲缝里立刻嵌了黑泥。炉子生起来后，屋里那股潮冷气慢慢退了，你才想起晚饭还没着落。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (赊煤到期还款含利息); food; flag +coal_credit_debt_

- **1947.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Mitzi听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Friedrich Hofer 2扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.06 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `p9-work-career.client-list/p9-work-career.client-list.referral` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, clients, reputation
  Mitzi把街角咖啡馆松动的八把椅子逐一套榫、重粘、上蜡，收了四克朗。老板让他下次过来喝咖啡时捎上小册子，当天就把楼下杂货铺和公寓看门人介绍给他。小册子上多了三行字，每个名字旁边写着“由某某介绍”。
  _effects: cash 4 (修咖啡馆八把椅子的工钱); reputation 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; skill_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

  她把签名纸送到市政厅，Otto Stein说格式不对，要按街道分开。她回去重抄，发现有几个名字已经认不出；再去时，窗口换了一个人，说前一班没有交接。她坐在市政厅台阶上重抄，风把纸吹到花坛里。
  _effects: flag +petition_refused; reputation 0.01999999999999999; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸的纸笔钱); health -0.010000000000000009 (来回跑市政厅后的疲惫); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分页，窗口换班时前一班可能没有交接”_

- **1949.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Mitzi没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007156_

- **1949.01** `task-vienna-medical-work.poor-patient/poor-patient-refused` tags: health, medicine, hospital, care, knowledge, profession, task, task:health
  Mitzi想起这家上次的诊费也没结，把处方推回去，只留下那张饮食单，说先照这个吃，药缓缓。Emil Weiss把硬币收回口袋，站在药房门口没走，最后把饮食单折成小方块捏在手心，回去的路上下起了雨。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1949.12** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.wring-blanket` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  毯子吸饱了水，沉得像一条死狗。Josef Weiss说这毯子是她婆婆的嫁妆，一年只洗一回。Mitzi咬着牙拧到最后一圈，水从毯缝里挤出来，在脚边汇成一小片温热。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (拧厚毯子把手勒得发酸); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.divorce::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government::divorce-reform/reform-divorce` tags: task, family, divorce, law, marriage, official, vienna, urban
  新的婚姻法把“感情破裂”写进了条文，Otto Stein却还在用旧表格。Mitzi和Elisabeth Huber被叫进一间小屋，问了半个钟头；Otto Stein最后说可以判，但要在报纸上登一条公告。公告费比手续费还贵，Mitzi还是把钱数了出来。
  _effects: cash -9 (登记簿上的注销线); condition; flag +divorce_under_reform_law; role; relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000872:child; knowledge “the new law requires a newspaper notice”_

  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸的纸笔钱); health -0.010000000000000009 (来回跑市政厅后的疲惫); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分页，窗口换班时前一班可能没有交接”_

- **1951.12** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Mitzi整场没上台，散会后留下把折叠椅叠好，扫掉地上的瓜子壳。Mitzi Lechner帮着把借来的煤气灯还给店主，顺手记下谁走得最早。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的人问Mitzi为何不发言，Mitzi说先把椅子还齐。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008030_

- **1952.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Mitzi和Rosa Stein合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000266:child; household; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1952.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Mitzi把报纸推到一边，和Maria Berg聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Mitzi起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002614:child; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  Mitzi拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (锥子扎伤拇指根); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.08** `r1-fam.inheritance-dispute::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/inheritance.lost` tags: family, inheritance, will, court, workshop, vienna, urban, postwar
  官司打了两年。Otto Stein每次开庭都换一条领带，Mitzi Lechner每次都坐在同一把椅子上。最后法院把工坊判给了Mitzi Lechner，Mitzi拿到一笔折价款，扣掉诉讼费只剩一半。Mitzi在工坊门口站了很久，最后把钥匙从钥匙圈上拧下来，放在窗台上。
  _effects: cash 5 (封蜡下面的名字); condition; flag +lost_the_family_workshop; role; relation rival with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “法院把工坊判给了年长的那个孩子。”_

- **1952.12** `r15-love.borrowed-room::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/begins` tags: love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi和Friedrich Hofer 2开始在咖啡馆后门见面。每次只有半个钟头，说的都是不能在家里说的话；Mitzi回家前会在街角站一会儿，把围巾重新系好，确认身上没有别人的烟味。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:67; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +secret_attachment_active_

  Mitzi把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Mitzi在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 10 -> 5; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 55 -> 58; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi替Mitzi Lechner把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Otto Stein翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Mitzi Lechner听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Mitzi替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”_

  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Josef被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Josef一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Lena提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Josef空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Katharina Bauer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Josef用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Josef把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Katharina Bauer。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; food; skill_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Josef后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Josef没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Josef用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1921.10** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/wooden-horse` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  木马从报纸里滚出来，落在Josef的膝盖上。Josef Bauer说是旧货摊上挑的，漆掉了一块，Josef用拇指摸了摸那块木头原色。那天Josef没舍得骑，只把木马放在枕边，半夜醒来还伸手摸了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.08; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Emil Weiss在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Emil Weiss数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1923.01** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Josef把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Franz Winter挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Franz Winter转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Josef站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000166:child_

  掌柜不肯赊，Rosa Berg从队尾挤过来，说这户人家的男人在印刷所做了十年，欠不了一筐煤。掌柜把“两赫勒”写在门牌号旁边，煤只装了小半筐。Josef抱着筐经过Rosa Berg身边，Rosa Berg替Josef把绳结紧了紧。

  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (搬纸捆扭了手腕); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  客户把床单贴在脸上，又摸了摸边角，说“还是潮的”，把门合上一半。Josef把床单卷回筐里，背回洗衣房。Gustav Berg没骂，只让在后院重晾，件钱等重送完再结。Josef蹲在地上翻布角，晚风吹得膝盖发酸，天黑前才夹完最后一只夹子。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.04; skill; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Josef把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Josef读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  Josef把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Helene Kral没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Josef回头看了一眼，Helene Kral正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1930.01** `major-task.relocation-klosterneuburg/moved-with-promise` tags: task, task:migration, migration, move, klosterneuburg, household, unemployment
  维也纳的房东把阁楼租金又涨了一成，Josef才下决心走。Josef在户籍办事处排了两个上午，把迁出证明和劳工证办齐。Gustav Berg在表格上盖了章，提醒到克洛斯特新堡后三天内去镇公所报到。月底，一家人把锅、被子和工具捆上手推车推到北站，坐火车到克洛斯特新堡，再借一辆手推车把东西推上坡，搬进姑妈家的阁楼；姑妈说葡萄园正缺短工，天亮先去问，不一定留下。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna workshop for Klosterneuburg); flag +vineyard_work_promised; cash -18 (train tickets, cart and attic rent（其中11.64克朗记入债务）); role; knowledge “迁出维也纳后要到镇公所登记，劳工证要随身带。”_

- **1930.10** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Helene Kral敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Helene Kral什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Friedrich Hofer来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle

  _effects: interaction_

  Gustav Berg把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Josef去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Elisabeth Stein 2给Josef一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Josef把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1942.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/crowd-tears-notices` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef刚把告示刷上墙，就有人围过来说这是拿疫病吓人。一个卖菜的女人伸手把纸撕下来，浆糊蹭了Josef一脸。Emil Weiss把Josef拉到一边，说这条街上个月才埋了两个孩子。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “the market crowd blames the notice for frightening customers”_

- **1942.11** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1942.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef先给Emil盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Emil吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Franz Winter 3在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Franz Winter 3在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood 0.03999999999999998; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）)_

- **1943.01** `r13-civ.arrange-move::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbors-carried` tags: social, housing, moving, landlord, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef没有雇车，把家什分摊给楼里的几户人家，一趟一趟往新住处搬。Katharina Bauer用童车推着锅碗，孩子在后面扶着。天黑前搬完，大家在空屋里分了一壶茶。
  _effects: cash -1 (板车与押金收据（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.05; flag +moved_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-katharina-bauer_

  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `r13-work.repay-debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pledge-seized` tags: commerce, debt, credit, obligation, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  还款日过了三天，Franz Winter 3带着Grete Stein 2上门，把Josef的缝纫机抬走了。Josef说再给一周，Franz Winter 3指着还款簿上的红格子说，规矩就是规矩。缝纫机被抬下楼时，踏板在墙上磕掉一块灰；Grete Stein 2站在门口，始终没有抬头。
  _effects: cash -8 (还款簿上的红格子（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +work13_debt_pledge_seized; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1944.02** `r3-crime.crime-scene::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::glass-and-blood/trampled-the-evidence` tags: crime, police, evidence, street, mistake, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef急着去看伤者，一脚踩进了粉笔线里，鞋底在血上滑了一下。Franz Winter 3蹲下去看了半天，说“这下只能靠口供了”。Ferdinand Stein递过来一块抹布，Josef没有接。那天晚上，局里传话说“现场保护不力”，名字写在通报的第二行。
  _effects: condition; flag +ruined_the_crime_scene; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; knowledge “the only shoe print was destroyed at the scene”_

- **1944.03** `r3-mig.refugee-column::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-written-for-free-water` tags: migration, refugees, column, hunger, flight, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef掏不出钱，Josef Kral还是给了半勺水，但拿出一个本子，让Josef把姓名和原籍写上。Josef问写这个做什么，Josef Kral说“以后有人问起来，我知道水给了谁”。本子上已经写了两页名字。
  _effects: food; flag +name_written_for_free_water; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “the farmer keeps a ledger of everyone he helped”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1944.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Gustav Berg 2把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Josef打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1944.05** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/hungry-winter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  Josef用配给卡换到半袋面粉和几根冻土豆。锅里煮的是稀汤，邻居送来一小块人造黄油，说孩子们先吃。
  _effects: food; condition_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Josef在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1945.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Josef把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Maria Bauer的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Josef就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Josef把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。
  _effects: cash -2 (第三遍换湿布（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +nursed_child_fever; health 0.05999999999999999 (the fever broke after a night of cold compresses)_

- **1945.02** `r1-cul.faith-crisis::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/confessed-doubt` tags: faith, doubt, confession, church, grief, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef在告解席前跪了很久，最后只说了一句：我不知道还有没有人在听。Marie Huber没有立刻回答，隔着帘子把一枚旧十字架放在窗台上。Gustav Berg 2在教堂门口等Josef，看见Josef出来时没有划十字，也没有问。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; condition; flag +confessed_doubt; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the priest left a worn cross on the window ledge”_

  Josef在工会活动室布置了一场没有十字架的葬礼，桌上放死者的工牌和一把扳手。Anna Bauer 2念完悼词，让大家把名字写在一块布上；Mitzi Hofer站在门口看了一会儿，没有进来，也没有走。
  _effects: mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +held_secular_funeral; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; knowledge “the funeral cloth carries the names of forty workers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1945.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.look-for-cheaper-bed` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Josef去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  Josef把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Katharina Moser扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Josef把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

- **1946.04** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.repair` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  Franz Winter 3从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Josef买双能进厂的结实靴子。Josef数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子); relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  Josef捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Josef Winter点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  书箱比看起来沉，Josef一个人抱下楼时膝盖直打颤。Marie Huber把午饭摆在新住处的窗台上，黑面包夹着咸肉，汤用旧罐子装着。工钱给的是铜币，数起来哗啦响。
  _effects: cash 3 (帮鞋匠搬家的工钱); health -0.019999999999999997 (搬重物腰背酸痛)_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Josef的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Josef看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Josef在巷口从一个戴鸭舌帽的人手里买了一张配给证，花了六克朗。证上的名字被刮过，纸面起毛。Mitzi收证时多看了一眼，还是打了孔。回家路上，Josef把证藏在鞋垫下面。
  _effects: cash -6 (配给证上的号码（其中4克朗记入债务）); food; flag +bought_black_market_ration_card; knowledge “the clerk noticed the scraped name but stamped the card anyw”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

  Josef请楼下的Ferdinand Moser 2白天来照看Katharina Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Ferdinand Moser 2上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Josef下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Katharina Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

  Luise Berg在井边哭，Josef假装没看见，把水桶放在她脚边就走了。第二天，Luise Berg端来一碗煮豆子，说昨天谢谢你没问。两人坐在楼梯上吃豆子，Luise Berg才说她丈夫又喝了酒；Josef说自己家的那个也一样。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +shared_husbands_drinking; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; knowledge “那个女人的丈夫喝酒打人。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Josef把欠账按日期抄在纸上，先还利息最高的一笔。Elisabeth Berg 2翻开米铺账本，把红字划掉一行，写“收到八克朗”。为了凑这笔钱，Josef把家里一只旧表送进当铺；Maria Hofer 2知道后，把自家的赊账往后挪了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (偿还米铺欠账（其中8克朗记入债务）); role; flag +debt_reduced; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; knowledge “米铺把赊账利息记在红字栏，先还红字最多的那笔最划算。”_

  Josef放弃存款，只拿走母亲的怀表；Emil答应替父亲还清杂货店的十一克朗。Otto Stein在清单上注了一笔，两人在走廊里站了一会儿，Emil说下个月去上坟。Josef把表上弦，表针走得很响。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +kept_family_watch; knowledge “兄弟答应替父亲还清杂货店的十一克朗。”_

  工厂只肯先签六周。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说干得好再续，干不好随时走。Josef点头，把这句话记在心里，也记住了门口那块写着“本厂不负责任何工伤”的牌子。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; employment factory worker @ org-winter-print; state_add factory-employment state:world-vienna-1912:56_

  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; food; condition_

  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。
  _effects: food; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  怀表是母亲陪嫁，表盖上有道划痕。Josef用指甲把表盖擦亮，说这个就够。兄弟把欠杂货店的账认下来，十一克朗。两人下楼时兄弟走在前面，到门口停了一下，说下个月一起去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.02** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Wien病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1957.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000266:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000266:child_

  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  Josef在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Josef拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Elisabeth Lechner找到Josef时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002825:child_

  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood -0.02999999999999997_

  Rosa从内袋里数出两克朗压在柜台上，Otto Winter 2翻开借书卡，用铅笔把上次迟还的四天圈出来，还是把小说递了过来。Rosa把书塞进外套里侧，出门就翻到第一页，边走边读，差点撞上推手推车的菜贩；两克朗换来的不只是一本小说，还有一整个下午的安静。
  _effects: cash -2 (租书店借小说的押金); skill; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1932.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.belt-patch` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Rosa拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: mood -0.03_

- **1932.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa上台只讲本街区：一间套房月租二十六克朗，三个失业的男人挤在一张床上。台下有人点头，有人低头看鞋。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的书记把数字记进小册子，主持人把发言时间多给了一分钟。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999999; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1933.01** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Rosa把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Friedrich Bauer放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Katharina Bauer在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Rosa挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Rosa侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1933.04** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Luise Kral把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Luise Kral在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1933.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Berta Lechner在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Berta Lechner敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1934.01** `r13-work.inherit-business::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/debts-came-with-it` tags: commerce, inheritance, family_firm, debt, succession, vienna, urban, crisis
  钥匙交到手里第二天，供货商就拿着欠条上门。Rosa Lechner说那些账是铺子的，不是她的；供货商说铺子现在归Rosa，账也归Rosa。Rosa把存货盘了一遍，能抵的抵，剩下十克朗写了还款计划；印章还在，但盖下去的第一份文件是欠条。
  _effects: cash -10 (父亲的账本和印章); condition; flag +work13_inherited_business_with_debts; role; employment Kaufmann @ org-wednesday-psychological-society_

- **1934.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Berta Berg听，Berta Berg摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.03_

- **1934.05** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Berta Weiss却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Rosa把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1935.01** `r3-crime.press-coverage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::two-sheet-paper/story-spiked` tags: crime, press, court, reputation, truth, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa把稿子里的名字换成了“某男子”，Franz Winter 2把版面撤下来，说“没有名字谁看”。Rosa坚持不改，第二天，桌子被清空，抽屉里的采访本被扔在走廊。Franz Berg 2后来托人送来一包烟，Rosa没有收。
  _effects: flag +story_spiked_to_protect_suspect; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; knowledge “the editor wanted a name before the verdict”; unemployment (dismissed for refusing to name a suspect before trial); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1935.01** `r13-work.ask-foreman::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/given-broom-and-number` tags: work, hiring, foreman, favour, queue, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Lechner 2正蹲在机器边上换皮带，听完没抬头，只把粉笔从耳朵后面取下来，在门框上写了个“七”。他说七点前来，先扫地、添油，工钱按杂工算。Rosa把那个数字记在手心，回家路上一直攥着拳头，怕汗把它抹掉。
  _effects: cash 1 (工头兜里的粉笔头); flag +work13_foreman_gave_a_start; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; employment Hilfsarbeiter @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1935.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Ferdinand Huber拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1936.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Ferdinand Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Rosa把床单对折，腾出一半，Ferdinand Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood 0.02_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003579_

- **1936.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Rosa在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Rosa的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1936.01** `r14-work.negotiate-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-for-eighteen` tags: debt, loan, money, institution, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  放贷人把借据推过来，第二行用更小的字写着月息。Rosa念到“逾期以家具作抵”时，Franz Winter 2把钢笔帽拧上，说这条街上没有第二家肯借钱给一个刚被停用的人。Anna Bauer 2在门口咳了一声，算是见证。Rosa签了名，十八克朗当场点清。
  _effects: cash 18 (借据上的第二行); condition; flag +took_loan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; knowledge “the loan is secured against household furniture”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1936.08** `r1-work.blacklist::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/placed-on-the-blacklist` tags: work, blacklist, unemployment, reputation, false_name, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Berg在名单上找到Rosa的名字，用红笔圈起来，在旁边写上“罢工期间活跃”。Rosa去应征时，工头一看名字就摇头。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的门房说没有空缺，可Rosa明明看见车间门口挂着招工牌。
  _effects: cash -6 (工厂联合会抽屉里的名单); flag +placed_on_employer_blacklist; role; relation rival with person-gustav-berg; unemployment (placed on the employer association blacklist); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1936.08** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Rosa手里。Luise Weiss的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Klara隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Luise Weiss再见到Rosa时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Rosa站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.08; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1936.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Rosa把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1937.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-returned` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban
  信过了三个星期被退回来，信封上盖着“查无此人”，边角已经磨毛。Katharina Bauer不认得那四个字，让Rosa念；Rosa念成“还在路上”，把信折好放进抽屉。Katharina Bauer点点头，第二天又问有没有回信。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); mood -0.12000000000000002; condition; flag +letter_returned_undeliverable; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1937.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Rosa额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Elisabeth Moser 2坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1937.11** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-look-only` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Rosa在人群外站到腿发酸，只隔着跳舞的人看了Otto Winter 2几眼。散场时Franz Winter裹着围巾凑过来，Rosa低声托Franz Winter去打听Otto Winter 2的门第和姓氏，自己先出了酒馆，在冷风里等回话；一整晚没敢上前，鞋底粘着酒馆地板的糖渍。
  _effects: mood -0.03_

- **1938.01** `r14-work.loan-default::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/half-a-year-more` tags: debt, loan, loan_default, law, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Winter 3把双方叫到楼下的门房，桌上摊着算到小数点后的账。Franz Winter 2同意再宽限半年，条件是Rosa每周多还一克朗，并在借据背面写清违约的后果。Luise Kral端来两杯水，杯底沉着茶叶。Rosa签字时，笔尖把“半年”两个字洇开了。
  _effects: cash -6 (到期日那天); condition; flag +loan_rescheduled; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; knowledge “the rescheduled loan costs one extra krone a week”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003900_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Gustav Berg在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Rosa排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `r13-edu.sell-books::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-old-textbooks` tags: education, books, sale, cash, study, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa把用过的课本捆成一摞，提到旧书摊。Ferdinand Moser一本本翻，把缺页的挑出来，最后给了五克朗。Rosa接过钱，看见自己写在页边的笔记被Ferdinand Moser随手撕掉，想说什么，还是没说，只把撕下的纸片捡了回来。纸片被油墨粘住，展开了。
  _effects: cash 5 (卖掉的课本); mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1938.04** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  汤锅冒着热气，Rosa盛出两勺倒进Emil Lechner带来的罐子里。Emil Lechner说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000189:child; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1939.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-morphine-trade` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  军需处先领走了奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林和泻盐。Rosa用一双旧靴子换了两支吗啡，药剂师把安瓿裹在报纸里，叮嘱别在街上打开。他把安瓿藏进帽衬，过检查站时低着头。
  _effects: cash -20 (boots and cash traded for two morphine ampoules); health 0.019999999999999997 (pain relief from a black-market ampoule); role; flag +traded_for_morphine; knowledge “军需处先领走奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林；旧靴子可以换两支吗啡。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1939.02** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa端着一只旧锅去敲Luise Kral的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Luise Kral让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Rosa连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; food; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1939.04** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa数出八赫勒交给Elisabeth Berg 2，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Elisabeth Berg 2说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Rosa盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1939.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Rosa把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1939.12** `r2-leisure.tarock-foursome::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::tarock-foursome-crisis/bet-bread-coupon-at-cards` tags: leisure, cards, tarock, gambling, etiquette, vienna, urban, crisis
  危机那年，塔罗克牌局的赌注从铜板变成面包券。Helene Kral说没钱的可以用配给券抵，Luise Kral说这不合规矩。Rosa把最后一张面包券押在桌上，牌发到一半，窗外有人排队领汤。Helene Kral把牌扣下，说这局不算。
  _effects: cash -2 (塔罗克牌局的第四个人); mood -0.07999999999999999; condition; flag +bet_bread_coupon_at_cards; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; knowledge “the card table now accepts bread coupons as stakes”_

- **1940.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Rosa的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Rosa被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1940.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Franz Berg 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1941.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa看Franz Berg 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Franz Berg 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Rosa门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Rosa打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状)_

  Rosa的房租也到期了，只好把Anton Winter 2的缝纫机搬到自己屋里，说等还钱再还机器。Anton Winter 2来取针线盒时，看见缝纫机头上盖着布，站了一会儿就走了；第二天，整栋楼都知道这件事。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; role; reputation -0.039999999999999994; flag +seized_sewing_machine; knowledge “缝纫机被扣作抵押，针线盒还留在邻居手里。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1941.02** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1941.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  Ferdinand Stein翻到担保人一栏，说必须有两位老会员签字。Mitzi Hofer愿意签一个，另一个名字写了又划掉。Ferdinand Stein把表推回来，说“不是不认老乡，是怕有人借了钱就走”。Rosa把一克朗会费收回口袋，听见里屋有人在分汤。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +refused_mutual_aid_membership; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; knowledge “membership requires two sponsors”_

- **1941.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1941.08** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Josefine Hofer却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Rosa把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1941.08** `statistical_recovery`

  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Rosa用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Anna Bauer 2翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Rosa Stein在走廊里问交了多少，Rosa含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.03999999999999998; reputation -0.02_

  军需处先领走了奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林和泻盐。Rosa用一双旧靴子换了两支吗啡，药剂师把安瓿裹在报纸里，叮嘱别在街上打开。他把安瓿藏进帽衬，过检查站时低着头。
  _effects: cash -20 (boots and cash traded for two morphine ampoules); health 0.019999999999999997 (pain relief from a black-market ampoule); knowledge “军需处先领走奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林；旧靴子可以换两支吗啡。”_

  负责人说这是不信任，Rosa回说数目对得上才叫信任。Anton Lechner把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000187:child; reputation 0.04; skill_

  _effects: cash 7 (租约上的第十三条); flag +sublet_room_without_landlord_consent; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; knowledge “the landlord will raise rent for the extra lodger”_

  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; flag +kept_off_choir_roll_for_safety; knowledge “the choir is being watched”_

  _effects: cash -20 (账单上的最后一行); condition; flag +medical_debt; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; knowledge “nursing and heating are billed separately from the operation”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

  _effects: condition; flag +carried_elder_on_night_march; relation friend with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the column must keep moving when trains pass”_

  _effects: cash 16 (保单背面的小字); flag +fire_claim_paid_with_rider; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; knowledge “the rider covers open-flame heating if the chimney is inspec”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”_

  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤)_

  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Emil把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Emil读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1930.08** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.poured-water` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  水从琴箱缝里淌下来，乐师用袖子擦着铜喇叭，嘴里嘟囔着谁也听不清的意大利话。Emil低头看自己的裤脚，泥点像撒上去的胡椒。旁边卖烤栗子的老头说，这条街的窗户最爱泼水。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1930.08** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Emil把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Alois Stein站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Emil手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1931.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.wait-for-the-brother` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Anna Kral把话说得很慢：母亲还在，弟弟的学徒期还有三年。Emil点头，把准备好的结婚证词折起来放回口袋，那是借教堂的旧纸写的。两人在电车站分开，约好明年复活节再谈。Anna Kral上车前替母亲拢了拢披肩，没有回头看。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +engagement_postponed; cash -1 (tram fare to the meeting); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Anna Kral的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Emil咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Emil手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Franz Stein等在门口，问值不值，Emil只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.08000000000000002; reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1931.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Josef Bauer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Josef Bauer敲开Emil的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  Luise Kral趁夜班把一版工会呼吁加薪的传单塞进Emil的排字盘里，说是替工友印的，谁都不会知道。Emil把那张纸压到排版最底层，机器照样轰隆转。
  _effects: flag +printed_union_pamphlet; relation colleague with person-luise-kral; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Emil在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Emil的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Helene Kral 2没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Emil回头看了一眼，Helene Kral 2正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Grete Kral帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Emil点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org-wien-magistrat; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; mood 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1932.03** `major-work-dismissal/leaves-before-dismissal` tags: work, dismissal, unemployment, major
  Emil在更衣室听见自己的名字被念到“下周停工”那一栏。没等账房把信封封好，Emil先开口说家里有事，要自己走。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Emil写一张自愿离职的纸，说这样双方都体面；Klara在门口摇头。走出厂门时，Emil的工票还在口袋里，辞工证明却没有。
  _effects: unemployment (left before the dismissal took effect); cash -5 (lost final settlement); role; flag +left_before_dismissal; relation colleague with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Emil没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002721_

  Emil听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Mitzi Hofer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1933.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Emil Weiss抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Emil Weiss用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/house-call-syrup-only` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, rural, agriculture, small_town
  医生没有来，只让药房送来一瓶止咳糖浆。Emil咳了三个星期，左肺留下杂音；Klara把窗户钉开一条缝，说新鲜空气比药便宜。后来他连上二楼都要停下来喘，空瓶在窗台上排成一排。瓶口结了一圈灰。
  _effects: cash -4 (cough syrup delivered instead of a visit); health -0.07999999999999996 (three weeks of untreated cough); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “请不到医生时，药房会送一瓶止咳糖浆；新鲜空气不要钱，肺里的杂音也不会自己消失。”_

- **1933.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.turned-back` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river, injury
  Emil走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个); health -0.010000000000000009 (在风口里走了一段，脸被吹得发红)_

  _effects: food; health -0.030000000000000027 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; mood 0.03999999999999998; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1937.02** `r13-rel.long-distance-marriage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::long-distance-marriage-war/field-post-arrived` tags: relationship, marriage, distance, letters, money, vienna, urban, crisis
  军邮隔了一个月才到，信封上盖着检查章的紫印。Anton Winter 2只写了两行：还活着，别等我。Emil把信读了三遍，第二天去邮局寄了一个包裹，里面是烟草、袜子和一张全家照。
  _effects: cash -3 (每月一封没有署名的信); flag +field_post_censored; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1937.07** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1937.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Emil Weiss听，Emil Weiss摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Franz Winter 3在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Emil排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/hurt` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick
  Emil在换班时被机器擦伤了手臂。工头让她去水龙头下面冲一冲，然后回来继续。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter factory-employment.injured false -> true_

- **1938.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-courtyard-room` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  他挑了朝院子的那间单间，付了一个月押金和半个月房租，房东点清钱才给钥匙。看门人把备用钥匙挂进门房的木板上，嘱咐他晚上十点以后别在楼梯上跺脚。屋里有张床、一只铁炉和半截蜡烛。
  _effects: cash -9 (单间一个月押金与半个月房租（其中7克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1938.03** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/take-to-hospital` tags: health, family, home
  Anton Stein的指头被冲床压住，Emil扶着一路走到医院。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne派人送来两克朗，指头保住了，却弯不回去；Anton Stein盯着自己的手，半天没说一句话。
  _effects: cash 2 (雇主给的医药补贴); reputation 0.05_

- **1938.03** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/mother-returns-to-work` tags: health, family, home, injury
  Katharina Bauer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Emil晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: health -0.05 (过早劳作导致腿伤加重); cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.full-bag` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil把一整袋面粉倒进Anton的空口袋，袋口用麻绳扎了两道，说等她男人发了薪再还。Anton连声道谢，把写着借煤日期的小纸片折好放回围裙。柜子里只剩一袋面粉，Emil当晚煮汤时多加了一勺水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Emil没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004321_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-wwii-conscription/wounded` tags: war, conscription, adulthood
  Emil在前线被弹片击中腿部，被送回维也纳的医院。Gustav Berg在站台上念了名字，Emil拄着拐杖下车，裤管空了一截。
  _effects: condition; role; cash 12 (military pension)_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/held-on` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick
  Emil又在机器边站满了一年。手指上的茧比去年硬，工钱没有涨。
  _effects: state_parameter factory-employment.seniority 1 -> 2_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.interview-worker::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/worker-talks` tags: knowledge, interview, worker, trust, journalism, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil在车间后面的煤堆旁拦住Maria Hofer，问加班费怎么算。Maria Hofer先看了看门口，才说计件单价去年就降了，工头把差额记在“工具损耗”里。Emil把三句话重复了一遍，确认没有听错。
  _effects: flag +interviewed_fellow_worker; skill; knowledge “the piece rate was cut and the difference booked as tool wea”_

- **1939.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Rosa Lechner 2从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1939.03** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.granted` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Emil在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的账房里向Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne开口预支十二克朗，因为家里的煤和面包钱同时到期。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne从抽屉里数出钞票，让会计在发薪日扣回，并记下“下不为例”。
  _effects: cash 12 (预支工资，先拿到现金); reputation 0.05000000000000002_

- **1939.05** `r1-cul.sports-match::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/winning-goal` tags: culture, sport, match, injury, bribe, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil在最后一分钟把球顶进门，鞋钉在泥里划出一道沟。Maria Hofer扑过来把Emil压在地上，观众席上有人把帽子扔进场。Helene Berg 2站在场边，等Emil爬起来才说：明天照常上工，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 1 (最后一分钟的球); reputation 0.06; flag +scored_winning_goal; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000964:child_

- **1940.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil看Rosa Lechner 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Rosa Lechner 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Emil门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Emil打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004555_

  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/pay-small-part` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。
  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1940.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Mitzi Hofer听，Mitzi Hofer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.06** `r1-cul.gambling-debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fled-to-the-station` tags: culture, gambling, debt, family, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil还不上钱，趁夜把工具装进麻袋，去车站买了一张最远的票。Gustav Berg 2第二天带人上门，Anton Winter 2说人已经走了；他们搬走了缝纫机和两把椅子，在墙上用粉笔写了一个日期。
  _effects: cash -6 (牌桌上的欠条（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +fled_gambling_debt; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; move place-wien; unemployment (left the shop to escape a gambling debt)_

- **1940.07** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004849_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:factory-employment` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick, state:factory-employment
  Emil的「工厂雇佣」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove factory-employment state:world-vienna-1912:17_

- **1941.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rejected-missing-book` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton说工龄册上少了最后三个月的盖章，不能算连续就业。Emil说厂里倒闭时没人盖章，Anton说那就去找厂里。厂门已经锁了，账房改成了仓库。Emil回到家，把工龄册一页页翻过，发现最后那页的纸比前面薄。
  _effects: cash -1 (补助金窗口的第二张表); mood -0.06999999999999984; flag +work13_benefit_rejected_missing_stamp; knowledge “the final three months of employment were never stamped”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1941.02** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-sell-furniture-move` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1941.06** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/bombed-out` tags: war, homefront, shortage, migration, injury
  半夜的空袭把屋顶掀开一角。Emil和邻居把家具拖到楼道，天亮后去市政办事处登记损失。窗户用木板钉上，一家人挤到地下室。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; health -0.019999999999999997 (air raid and cold); condition; cash -6 (repairs and moving（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1941.08** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.repair` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  Anton Winter 2从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Emil买双能进厂的结实靴子。Emil数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子); relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1941.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.upstairs` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Emil拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

- **1942.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005069_

- **1942.01** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-watched-tools` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  河面的雾散尽时，码头上的人走了一半。Emil蹲在石阶上替Mitzi Moser守着麻绳和铁钩，看装卸工在跳板上喊号子。汤送来时还烫嘴，豆子沉在碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  Elisabeth Stein说Emil的工龄不够，或者离开的方式算“自愿”，两条都不符合。Emil问哪一条，Elisabeth Stein把表格转过来，用手指点了点空白处。走廊里有人抱着孩子排队，孩子在哭。Emil把号码牌放在窗台上，走了出去。
  _effects: cash -1 (失业救济的号码牌（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +benefit_denied; knowledge “a voluntary departure disqualifies the claim”_

- **1942.03** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-a-secret` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, crisis
  Marie Huber把Emil在饭桌上说过的名字和地址卖给了别人。几天后，Emil发现自己的话出现在一封不该出现的信里。Marie Huber没有否认，只说“大家都要活”。
  _effects: flag +betrayed_after_trusting; relation rival with person-marie-huber; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Emil把免役申请递进窗口，理由是家里只有Emil一个男劳力，Josef Bauer的腿又不好。Franz Winter 3翻到第二页，说“地主签字不算”，要村公所盖章。Emil Weiss在门外等，手里拎着一篮鸡蛋，说章可以等，麦子不能等。
  _effects: flag +appeal_requires_village_seal; relation colleague with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “the exemption needs a village office seal”_

- **1942.12** `r1-civ.refugee-hostel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/registered-as-political-refugee` tags: migration, exile, refugee, work, language, vienna, urban, crisis
  难民旅馆的登记簿上，Franz Winter 3让Emil写原国籍和“离境原因”，Emil写了“经济”，Franz Winter 3划掉，改成“政治”。房间在四楼，六个人一间，窗对着天井。Rosa从上铺探下头，问Emil会不会说本地话，Emil说只会几个词。
  _effects: cash -2 (难民旅馆的登记簿（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +registered_as_political_refugee; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the hostel clerk rewrites economic departure as political ex”_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Emil谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 30_

- **1943.01** `kernel-migrate.return-home::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-return-refused` tags: migration, return, border, family, war, vienna, urban, crisis
  他被拒绝入境，理由是“没有固定住所”。他在边境站给Josef打电话，电话转到邻居家；对方说“先回来再说”，他买了一张反向车票。他在边境站的长椅上坐到天亮，才买下反向车票。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -5 (反向车票和电话费); flag +border_refused; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000503:child; condition; knowledge “没有固定住址会被拒绝入境，亲戚的电话不能作为住址证明”_

- **1943.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/give-camp-bed-and-board` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil让Franz Berg睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Emil把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); food; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +kin_staying_over; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  对方的律师带来了一个信封，说“人已经没了，别再让孩子跟着受罪”。Josef把信封推回去，Emil却伸手按住了。Maria Hofer在门外咳嗽了一声。那天晚上，Josef没有跟Emil说话，只把灵位前的供饭倒进了垃圾桶。
  _effects: cash 7 (灵堂里那张没写完的状纸); condition; flag +accepted_settlement_for_a_death; relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the settlement came with a promise of no admission”_

- **1943.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Anton Lechner追出来问怎么不领了，Emil说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Emil把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: food_

- **1943.07** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop

  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/ledger-on-table` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Josef的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Emil在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: flag +medicine_bill_settled_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Emil住的那条街烧掉一半。Emil跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1945.01** `r14-soc.gamble-away-wages::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/won-fifteen` tags: task, gambling_debt, gambling, money, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil押的那匹马在最后一个弯道超了过去。Berta Hofer把十五克朗拍在柜台上，说“运气不错”。Emil请Gustav Berg 2喝了半升啤酒，剩下的钱在口袋里响了一路。第二天，Emil又去了赛马场。那匹马的号码被Emil写在烟盒上，后来烟盒被雨淋烂了，号码还认得出。回家后，Emil把那张湿透的烟盒摊在炉边烘干。
  _effects: cash 15 (第三个栏架前的马); mood 0.19999999999999996; condition; flag +gambling_winnings; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1945.05** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/bombed-out` tags: war, homefront, shortage, migration
  半夜的空袭把屋顶掀开一角。Emil和邻居把家具拖到楼道，天亮后去市政办事处登记损失。窗户用木板钉上，一家人挤到地下室。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; cash -6 (repairs and moving（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.08** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/mother-returns-to-work` tags: health, family, home
  Katharina Bauer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Emil晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.10** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/walk-home` tags: health, family, home, injury
  Emil把Otto Bauer 2扶回地下室，用干净布条裹住伤指。家里人连声道谢，Emil心里却盘算明天怎么跟Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说；布条很快洇出一点红。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128; health -0.030000000000000027 (伤口只经简单包扎); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128_

  “十克朗押金，先交后干。”Maria Winter说得很平。Emil把两只口袋翻给人家看，柜台上只落下一张皱车票。工头收回话头，他也不再问，提着帽子走出栅栏。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi Hofer把三块无酵饼用白纸包好，说今年面粉紧，每家只能领这些。Emil看见纸包上印着“救济会”，边角还有一个没擦干净的地址。回家路上，Marie Huber问够不够做主日餐，Emil说够了，其实心里在算人数。
  _effects: food; flag +received_passover_matzo; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; knowledge “the matzo parcels are packed at the welfare office on Franze”_

  Emil在院子的水龙头边等到Hermann Stein，把那天酒桌上的话重复了一遍，没有找补。Hermann Stein拧上水龙头，说那句话已经传到第三个院子了。Emil问要怎样才能收回，Hermann Stein说收不回，但可以当着传话的人再说一遍是胡说的。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +apology_accepted; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002154:child; knowledge “the insult has already reached three courtyards”_

  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子); relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_
